This paper proposes a multi-agent deep reinforcement learning framework that addresses these issues through coordinated exploration, demonstration exploitation, safe curriculum scheduling, and structure-aware generalisation. First, a perception mechanism combining memory of visited states, directional novelty estimates, and penalty backpropagation enables agents to proactively detect and escape local optima. Second, a hierarchical collaborative demonstration buffer with tiered behaviour cloning manages trajectories by degree of team collaboration and applies differential supervision to the actor network, improving demonstration utilisation under sparse collaborative signals. Third, a safety-aware dual-condition curriculum scheduling mechanism reviews mastered scenarios through back-testing and experience pre-filling during training, suppressing catastrophic forgetting while ensuring both task performance and flight safety. For generalisation, local geometric features computed from sensor readings are abstracted into a domain parameter, through which a structure-aware gating network and mixture-of-experts mechanism condition the policy on local structural patterns rather than scenario-specific coordinates, enabling cross-scenario transfer without exposure to the target environment. The framework is further validated under mixed static-dynamic obstacle settings, showing robust adaptability to dynamic disturbances. Simulation results confirm strong performance in collaboration success rate, navigation robustness, zero-shot cross-scenario generalisation, and dynamic environment adaptability.

</details>


### [110] [Tripody: An Overconstrained 3-SPR-like Parallel Robot for High-Reach Construction Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25781)
*Julien Kindle,Jakub Raczy,Riccardo Balbi,Andrea Alessandretti,Cesar Cadena,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Tripody，一种轮式3自由度并联机器人，通过将经典3-SPR机构的基座球铰替换为万向节实现过约束设计，利用分布式弹性变形吸收运动不兼容性。该机器人重33kg，工作高度1.7-3.4m，支持32kg负载，在实验中展现出比球铰变体高67%-454%的扭转刚度，闭环定位精度低于0.6mm，并成功完成了天花板钻孔任务验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统天花板施工作业依赖重型串联机械臂，在杂乱室内环境中部署困难，需要一种轻量级、可现场部署、兼具毫米级精度和高刚度的替代方案。

Method: 采用过约束3-SPR构型（将基座球铰替换为万向节），通过分布式弹性变形吸收运动学不兼容性，结合SE(3)状态估计、正向运动学和任务空间控制实现精确定位。

Result: 相比球铰变体，扭转刚度提升67%（1.7m）、196%（2.6m）和454%（3.4m）；闭环定位误差<0.6mm；模型外推95百分位误差2.7mm（最大3.6mm）；钻孔任务最大相对孔位误差4.5mm。

Conclusion: 过约束、顺应性吸收的3-SPR类架构是实现轻量、高可达、毫米级精度施工机器人的可行路径。

Abstract: Many ceiling construction tasks still rely on heavy serial manipulators that are difficult to deploy in cluttered interiors, motivating lightweight, field-ready alternatives that reach ceiling height while maintaining millimeter-level accuracy and the stiffness demanded by overhead tool loads. We introduce Tripody, a wheeled 3-DoF parallel robot for high-reach tasks that replaces the base spherical joints of a classical 3-SPR (3 legs; S: base spherical joint; P: actuated prismatic joint; R: end-effector revolute joint) morphology with universal joints, intentionally overconstraining the mechanism; small, distributed elastic deflections absorb the resulting incompatibilities, preserving predominantly translational motion. The 33kg system extends from 1.7m to 3.4m in height, supports a continuous 32kg payload, and offers a modular end-effector interface for ceiling operations. We detail the mechanical design - including custom linear actuators and a kinematic-compatibility analysis - and a control stack for accurate positioning that combines SE(3) state estimation, forward kinematics, and task-space control. In experiments, Tripody exhibits similar in-plane stiffness to a spherical-base variant but substantially higher torsional stiffness - an increase of 67% at 1.7m, 196% at 2.6m, and 454% at 3.4m - while maintaining negligible cross-axis coupling. Closed-loop positioning with a total station converges below 0.6mm across the entire workspace; pure model extrapolation achieves a 95th-percentile error of 2.7mm (max 3.6mm). Finally, we demonstrate task-level ceiling-drilling feasibility in an open-loop study by drilling a 15-hole pattern with 4.5mm maximum relative hole-position error after rigid alignment. These results support overconstrained, compliance-absorbing 3-SPR-like architectures as a practical path to lightweight, high- reach, millimeter-accurate construction robots.

</details>


### [111] [Motion-Acceleration Calibration and Compensation in IMUs without External Equipment for Attitude Estimation Filters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25784)
*Fabian Arzberger,Andreas Nüchter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出利用陀螺仪测量值补偿由运动引起的额外加速度（向心加速度和切向加速度），并引入无需外部设备的IMU内外参数标定方法，从而提升依赖重力方向的姿态估计算法的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在运动系统中，IMU远离旋转中心时会受到额外加速度干扰，导致重力测量失真；现有滤波方法在高速运动下依赖陀螺仪积分易累积长期误差，特别是在重力方向附近。

Method: 利用陀螺仪测量值补偿向心加速度和切向加速度；提出一种无需外部设备的标定方法，估计IMU内参（轴偏差、偏置、尺度）及外参（基座到IMU向量）。

Result: 在仿真和真实实验中，该方法显著提升了依赖重力方向的姿态滤波器的性能，并在高动态系统及无法将IMU置于旋转中心的系统中验证了有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法可推广至任何依赖重力方向的姿态滤波器中，尤其适用于高动态运动和IMU远离旋转中心的场景。

Abstract: Attitude estimation based on inertial sensing requires measurements of local angular velocities and local gravity via gyroscopes and accelerometers.
  However, during the motion of a mobile system the inertial measurement unit (IMU) will be subject to additional accelerations which skews the measurement of local gravity.
  This effect gets amplified the further away the IMU is from the base of the system.
  Many attitude estimation filters, such as "Madgwick" or "Mahony", account for this by relying more on gyroscope integration for periods of high angular velocity.
  However, this approach is prone to accumulate long term error especially around the gravity vector.
  In this work we utilize the gyroscope measurements to compensate the additional accelerations induced by the motion of the system, i.e., centripetal- and tangential-accelerations.
  Additionally, we introduce a calibration method that estimates intrinsic IMU parameters such as axes misalignment, bias, scale, as well as the extrinsic base-to-IMU vector without the necessity for additional external equipment.
  Our evaluation in simulation as well as in the real-world shows that this method improves any attitude filter that relies on the direction of gravity.
  Furthermore we demonstrate the effectivenes on highly dynamic systems, and systems that are unable to put the IMU at the center of rotation, using our real-world spherical mobile mapping system.

</details>


### [112] [Transformer Transformer: A Unified Model for Motion-Conditioned Robot Co-design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25798)
*Huy Ha,C. Karen Liu,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Transformer Transformer，一个基于扩散Transformer的机器人协同设计框架，通过动力学自引导机制从人类示教轨迹生成优化机器人本体设计，在多个设计空间实现零样本奖励优化，并将ALOHA机械臂的跟踪误差降低70%以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人本体形态（embodiment）是影响操作性能的关键因素但常被忽视，现有方法往往针对单一奖励函数过拟合，缺乏跨形态、跨任务的泛化能力。

Method: 提出RoboTokens统一tokenization框架（编码本体、状态、动作），训练扩散Transformer作为奖励无关的动力学模型；通过Dynamics Self-Guidance利用模型的价值预测引导本体扩散过程生成高价值设计。

Result: 在多个设计空间上实现零样本优化未见过的奖励和轨迹，性能与运行时间均超越进化基线；优化后的ALOHA设计跟踪误差相比原始设计降低超过70%。

Conclusion: 奖励无关的动力学模型+扩散引导范式能够有效泛化到新任务和新形态，为机器人协同设计提供了一种统一的、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: An often overlooked factor of robot manipulation performance is the embodiment of the robot itself. Motivated by this problem, we study motion-conditioned robot co-design, where the goal is to generate complete robot designs that track target end-effector trajectories (from human demonstrations) while optimizing user-defined rewards. We introduce Transformer Transformer, a diffusion transformer trained on RoboTokens, a unified tokenization of robot embodiments, states, and actions. The same architecture can be used across embodiment spaces (e.g., wheeled bimanual, quadrupeds, humanoids) and use cases (embodiment generation, cross embodiment controller). Rather than overfitting to one reward function, Transformer Transformer is a dynamics model, whose reward-agnostic state and action predictions can be converted into reward-specific value predictions. These value predictions are used to steer embodiment diffusion towards high value robot designs, through a procedure we call Dynamics Self-Guidance. Experiments across multiple design spaces show zero-shot optimization of unseen rewards and trajectories, improving performance and runtime over the evolutionary baseline. Finally, we fabricated an optimized ALOHA design, which reduced tracking error by over 70% compared to the original design.

</details>


### [113] [Modular Robotic Catheters for Endovascular Aneurysm Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25807)
*Alex Ranne,Jinshi Zhao,Ali Anil Demircali,Songli Moey,Ayhan Aktas,Burak Temelkuran,Nassir Navab,Ferdinando Rodriguez y Baena*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于F/B-EVAR手术的2段式可操控导管系统（4自由度+模块化驱动平台），采用热拉伸光纤制造和激光微加工尖端软化技术，通过仿真和体外实验验证了其弯曲刚度和可操控性，有望缩短手术时间并解决复杂临床病例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有F/B-EVAR手术中，医生使用标准器械进行血管分支导航时存在操作困难、手术时间长、并发症多等问题，需要更灵巧的导管系统来改善血管通路。

Method: 1) 设计2段式可操控导管（4自由度），提升灵活性；2) 开发可扩展的模块化腱驱动平台，适应系统冗余；3) 采用热拉伸光纤制造技术实现规模化生产，结合激光微加工软化导管尖端。

Result: 通过仿真评估了导管的弯曲刚度，并在血管体模中进行了体外可操控性实验验证。

Conclusion: 该手持式机器人可操控导管系统有潜力缩短未来腔内手术时间，并为临床医生提供解决复杂病例的工具。

Abstract: Fenestrated/Branched endovascular aneurysm repair (FEVAR/BEVAR) require surgeons to navigate catheters and guidewires into various branches of the abdominal aorta, before deploying stent grafts to alleviate pressure on the aneurysm. Previous clinical studies suggests that surgeons continue to struggle with vessel access using standard commercial instruments, prolonging the procedural time and inducing further complications. In this work, we present two contributions to solving this problem: 1) A bespoke 2-segment steerable catheter, consisting of 4 degrees of freedom to enhance dexterity. 2) An expandable, modular tendon-driven actuation platform that can accommodate for the redundancies introduced in our system. To fabricate the catheter, we capitalized on thermal fiber drawing, a technique that creates high-aspect ratio devices at scale, and processed the catheter with laser micro-machining to soften its tip. We evaluated the system using simulations, where we investigated the catheter's bending stiffness, then its steerability with in-vitro experiments in vascular phantoms. This handheld, robotic steerable catheter system has the potential to shorten the length of future endovascular surgeries, and give clinicians the tools to resolve challenging clinical cases.

</details>


### [114] [HiFi-UMI: Learning Deployable Manipulation Policies from High-Fidelity UMI Data Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25895)
*Simple AI,:,Yuteng Wei,Jinming Ma,Jiawei Wang,Weitao Zhou,Yushen Zuo,Ke Rui,Minglei Li,Jinhao Zhang,Zhikang Pan,Xiang Wang,Haoran Jia,Huan Du,Zicheng Zeng,Jun Ma,Guiyu Qin,Di Zhang,Xiaofei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出HiFi-UMI，一种高保真无机器人数据采集系统，通过提升无机器人UMI数据采集的精度（3mm末端精度、微秒同步、200°广角），实现了无需真实机器人微调即可直接部署策略，在三个基础模型上表现与遥操作基线相当，且大规模预训练进一步提升了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人操作策略学习受限于数据的高保真度与可扩展性之间的矛盾：真实机器人遥操作精度高但成本高昂难以规模化，而无机器人UMI数据虽然易于大规模采集，但精度不足，通常只能用于预训练，仍需少量真实数据微调。本文旨在通过提升无机器人UMI数据的精度，消除对真实机器人微调的依赖。

Method: 提出HiFi-UMI数据采集系统，关键设计包括：(1) 头戴式离线立体惯性SLAM实现高精度轨迹估计；(2) 原生而非重建的夹爪间相对位姿；(3) 共享微秒级GPIO触发实现精确同步；(4) 每只手配备两个广角相机覆盖约200°视野。该系统无需外部追踪基础设施即可达到3mm工作空间内末端执行器精度。

Result: (1) 零机器人后训练：仅使用HiFi-UMI数据微调的策略可直接部署在真实机器人上，与遥操作基线相比成功率差异仅为-2.5、+3.1、-0.6个百分点（三个基础模型）；最强策略在精密插入任务上达到85%成功率。(2) 大规模预训练：在4000小时HiFi-UMI数据上预训练使10个未见任务的动作误差降低41%，在StarVLA-QwenPI上真实机器人成功率再提升18.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 高保真的无机器人数据采集是可行的，足以替代遥操作数据用于策略微调，同时支持大规模预训练。开源HiFi-UMI-2K（2000小时微秒同步、超广角演示数据）为机器人学习社区提供了大规模高保真资源。

Abstract: Learning deployable manipulation policies is bottlenecked by the scarcity of data that is both high-fidelity and scalable. Real-robot teleoperation is accurate but costly to scale; robot-free UMI capture scales readily, and current practice uses the resulting data mainly for pre-training, adding a small real-robot "anchor" at post-training. We ask whether raising the fidelity of robot-free UMI data, rather than shrinking the real-robot fraction, can remove that anchor. We present HiFi-UMI, a portable UMI data-production system co-designed for trajectory accuracy, inter-gripper relative pose, synchronization, and field of view: head-mounted offline stereo-inertial SLAM, native rather than reconstructed relative pose, a shared microsecond GPIO trigger, and two wide-angle cameras per hand covering ~200 degrees. It reaches 3 mm workspace-local end-effector accuracy without external tracking infrastructure. Using this corpus, we demonstrate zero-robot post-training: a policy post-trained solely on HiFi-UMI demonstrations deploys directly on a real robot and matches in-domain teleoperation across three backbones spanning the vision-language-action and world-action-model families, with success-rate differences of -2.5, +3.1, and -0.6 percentage points on StarVLA-QwenPI, OpenPI-pi_0.5, and LingBot-VA; the strongest policy reaches 85% on a precision insertion task, even though the teleoperation baseline is collected in the evaluation scene and no HiFi-UMI trajectory is. Pre-training on 4,000 hours from the same corpus lowers action error on ten unseen tasks by 41% and, on StarVLA-QwenPI, raises real-robot success by a further 18.1 percentage points. We open-source HiFi-UMI-2K, 2,000 hours of microsecond-synchronized, ultra-wide-FoV demonstrations, each automatically reconstructed and validated through simulation replay, as a large-scale, high-fidelity resource for the robot-learning community.

</details>


### [115] [SAM3D-Guided Object-Centric Representation Alignment for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25912)
*Zonghe Liu,Shanyuan Jie,Xiaoquan Sun,Chen Cao,Zetian Xu,Zongsheng Liu,Jiayu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个基于π₀的目标中心3D表示对齐框架，利用SAM3D作为冻结教师模型提供3D先验，在不增加测试时计算负担的情况下显著提升机器人操作性能，在LIBERO上达99.1%，CALVIN平均长度4.11。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型主要依赖2D视觉语言骨干，缺乏对目标物体的细粒度3D理解能力，尤其在遮挡、姿态变化、尺度变化和精确空间交互场景下表现不佳。

Method: 以π₀为基础，训练时用目标检测模型定位任务相关物体并生成掩码，SAM3D（冻结）提取密集3D表示，与π₀中间视觉特征对齐；测试时无需深度图、点云、SAM3D等额外模块。

Result: 仿真实验：LIBERO达99.1%，CALVIN平均长度4.11。真实实验：在长程操作场景中效果显著，机器人需在多子任务中关注不同目标物体。

Conclusion: 通过训练时对齐3D目标表示，可在不增加测试时开销的情况下提升VLA模型的3D理解能力，尤其有利于长程多目标操作任务。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong potential for general robot manipulation, but most existing models rely on 2D visual-language backbones and lack fine-grained 3D understanding of target objects, especially under occlusion, pose variation, scale changes, and precise spatial interaction. We propose an object-centric 3D representation alignment framework built upon $π_0$, using SAM3D as a frozen 3D teacher to provide target-object 3D priors during training. Specifically, we localize task-relevant objects with object recognition models, generate corresponding object masks, and use SAM3D to extract dense object-level 3D representations, which are aligned with intermediate visual features of $π_0$. This enables the policy to internalize target-object 3D information while preserving the original RGB-language-to-action inference pipeline without requiring depth, point clouds, masks, SAM3D, or additional 3D modules at test time. Simulation experiments show consistent improvements, achieving 99.1\% on LIBERO and an average length of 4.11 on CALVIN. Real-world experiments further demonstrate that our method is particularly effective in long-horizon manipulation scenarios where the robot must focus on different target objects across multiple subtasks.

</details>


### [116] [DC-WAM: Dynamic-Centric Visual Supervision and Reasoning for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25918)
*Haoyuan Ji,Lingxiang Fan,Shang Su,Yinqiao Lu,Mengkai Shi,Jun Gao,Shuo Feng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DC-WAM框架，通过动态中心视角重新设计未来视频预测的监督和推理机制，引导视觉模型关注控制相关的动态变化而非外观重建，从而提升策略在分布外场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有World-Action Models（WAM）依赖未来视频预测进行策略增强，但像素级重建耗费大量计算资源，且模型容易过度学习纹理、光照、背景等与动作选择弱相关的视觉信息，导致在分布外场景下性能下降。

Method: DC-WAM在RGB视频分支上从两个层面改进：(1) 监督层面：提出时序差分流匹配和轨迹引导加权，强调密集时序变化和操作相关局部区域；(2) 推理层面：设计DynaRoute预测token级别的动态相关性并转化为注意力偏置，引导模型关注控制相关的未来token。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作任务上，DC-WAM持续提升策略性能，尤其在光照、物体外观和背景纹理等分布外扰动下表现显著优于基线。

Conclusion: 未来视频预测的主要收益不在于渲染的未来帧本身，而在于训练过程中诱导出的控制相关视觉表征；将计算和监督从外观重建重定向到交互引发的动态变化，是提升策略鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: World-Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies with future visual prediction, but it remains unclear what the visual modality should learn for control. While photorealistic future prediction provides dense supervision, it also incurs substantial computation and can allocate capacity to texture, illumination, and background variations that are only weakly related to action selection. Recent efficient WAM variants suggest that the main benefit of the video branch may not lie in the rendered future itself, but in the control-relevant visual representations induced during training. In this work, we revisit future video prediction from a dynamic-centric perspective and ask whether an existing RGB-based WAM can be redirected from appearance-dominated reconstruction toward interaction-induced visual dynamics without introducing additional modality-specific predictions or online inputs at deployment. We propose DC-WAM, a dynamic-centric WAM framework that redistributes supervision and computation in the RGB video branch. At the supervision level, DC-WAM combines temporal-difference flow matching with trajectory-guided weighting, emphasizing dense temporal changes and localized regions where the gripper, manipulated objects, and contact areas move. At the reasoning level, DynaRoute predicts token-wise dynamic relevance and converts it into an attention bias, guiding the model toward control-relevant future tokens. Experiments in simulation and on real-world manipulation tasks show that DC-WAM consistently improves policy performance, especially under out-of-distribution perturbations in lighting, object appearance, and background texture.

</details>


### [117] [Physics-Aware End-to-End Deep Reinforcement Learning for Quadcopter Control with Actuator Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.25985)
*Ya-Chia Shen,Woei-Leong Chan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对四旋翼无人机欠驱动动力学特性，采用物理感知的端到端深度强化学习方法，在包含执行器动力学和陀螺耦合的高保真Simulink环境中，比较四种DRL算法（DDPG、TD3、PPO、SAC）的低层控制性能，发现SAC和TD3在稳定性和探索效率上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四旋翼无人机具有欠驱动特性（4个输入控制6个自由度），传统控制方法难以应对复杂环境。需要一种端到端的、物理感知的深度强化学习方法来实现自主稳定控制，并解决执行器滞后和气动力矩建模问题。

Method: 构建12状态刚体模型的Simulink高保真仿真环境，集成Action2RPM分配（基于Moore-Penrose伪逆）、一阶执行器动力学（时间常数0.076s）及转子陀螺耦合。设计包含指数位置阱、姿态惩罚和二次速度代价的奖励函数。在两种场景（仅推力悬停、含俯仰力矩的平移悬停）下评估DDPG、TD3、PPO、SAC四种算法。

Result: SAC和TD3在稳定性和探索效率上优于DDPG和PPO；PPO样本效率较低；建模执行器滞后和气动力矩对低层控制稳定性至关重要。

Conclusion: 物理感知的DRL方法可有效解决四旋翼欠驱动控制问题，SAC和TD3是较优的选择，且高保真仿真中纳入执行器动力学对稳定控制具有重要意义。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs), particularly quadcopters, present unique challenges for autonomous control due to their underactuated dynamics: only four available control inputs must govern six degrees of freedom. This paper investigates a physics-aware, end-to-end deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approach that acts directly on low-level body inputs, total thrust and body torques $(T, τ_x, τ_y, τ_z)$, and closes the loop through a high-fidelity Simulink environment. Our simulator integrates a 12-state rigid-body model (MATLAB Level-2 S-Function) with (i) an Action2RPM allocation based on the Moore-Penrose pseudo-inverse of a coefficient matrix derived from thrust and drag terms, and (ii) first-order actuator dynamics for each motor (time constant $T_m = 0.076$ s), including rotor gyroscopic coupling. A shaped reward balances goal-reaching and stability using an exponential position well, attitude penalties, and quadratic velocity costs. Four DRL algorithms, DDPG, TD3, PPO, and SAC, are evaluated in two stages: (S1) thrust-only hover and (S2) hover with pitch torque and a translated goal. Results show that SAC and TD3 achieve superior stability and exploration efficiency, while PPO is less sample-efficient. The study highlights the significance of modeling actuator lags and aerodynamic moments for stable low-level control and provides a reproducible benchmark for quadcopter DRL.

</details>


### [118] [S2A2: Audio-Visual Imitation Learning for Manipulation Tasks Using Acoustic Spatial Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26047)
*Kaneyoshi Hiratsuka,Benjamin Yen,Ryosuke Kojima*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套基于音频感知的机器人模仿学习任务，并设计了多模态框架S2A2，融合视觉、声学空间和声学信号信息，在仿真和真实实验中均验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 声学信息能提供物体位置、材质属性及接触运动变化等丰富线索，但现有机器人操作任务大多依赖视觉，缺乏对听觉信息的利用。

Method: 提出多模态模仿学习框架S2A2，融合视觉特征、声学空间信息和声学信号信息，并将ACT、Diffusion Policy、VQ-BeT、π₀等策略集成到该框架中。

Result: 仿真实验表明该方法在同时需要位置和音色判断的任务中效果最优；真实机器人实验验证了任务和框架在现实操作中的可行性。

Conclusion: 融合声学信息的机器人操作任务具有可行性，S2A2框架能有效利用听觉线索提升机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Acoustic information provides rich cues about object location, material properties, and changes caused by contact or motion. This paper introduces a new set of acoustic-aware manipulation tasks for imitation learning, in which robots must use auditory cues to determine manipulation targets. These tasks require sound source localization and identification for active exploration in robotic manipulation. Also, we propose a multimodal imitation learning framework, Spatial-Spectral Audio Action (S2A2), that integrates visual features with acoustic spatial and acoustic signal information for the acoustic-aware manipulation tasks. We implemented S2A2 models that integrates policies such as ACT, Diffusion Policy, VQ-BeT, and $π_0$, into our framework. Simulation experiments showed that the proposed method is the most effective for tasks requiring both position and timbre. Furthermore, real-robot experiments confirm the applicability of the proposed tasks and framework to real-world manipulation.

</details>


### [119] [$π\mathbf{R}^2$: Reactive Real-time Flow Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26055)
*Sungjae Park,Shubham Tulsiani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出πR²方法，通过快慢双通道条件机制和延迟自适应流调度，使基于大模型的动作分块策略能够在保持实时性的同时实现闭环反应控制，在仿真和真实环境中分别提升成功率高达23%和30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大模型的动作分块策略存在两大问题：一是开环执行导致无法响应执行过程中的感官反馈（缺乏反应性）；二是感知到动作的完整pipeline（大模型+多步去噪）延迟过高，无法频繁重规划，使得策略难以应用于动态场景的闭环控制。

Method: 基于扩散强制的位置相关噪声调度，提出两个核心机制：(1) 快慢双通道条件机制——快通道处理每个时间步更新的本体感知，慢通道异步更新视觉-语言特征，使策略在块内响应本体感知；(2) 延迟自适应流调度——将在途动作视为修复条件，每次调用仅需一步去噪生成动作，一个模型可适应不同硬件延迟。

Result: 在真实xArm6+XHand平台上，基于GR00T-N1.7微调，实现约25Hz的闭环重规划（比基线快约4倍），每40ms接收一次新观测。仿真任务提升成功率高达23%，真实任务提升30%。

Conclusion: πR²以最小化架构修改即可从预训练策略微调，在保持大模型表达能力和多动作预测的同时，显著提升了动作分块策略的反应性和实时性，使其适用于动态场景的闭环控制。

Abstract: Generalist manipulation policies increasingly take the form of action-chunking flow policies built on large pretrained backbones. Such chunks run open-loop, so the policy cannot react to sensory input arriving mid-execution, sacrificing \emph{reactivity}. Replanning more often would restore it, but the perception-to-action pipeline (a large backbone plus multiple denoising steps) is too slow: this \emph{latency} forbids frequent replanning and leaves committed actions stale, making such policies ill-suited for dynamic, closed-loop control. We present $π\mathbf{R}^2$, which makes these policies reactive and real-time while retaining large backbones, expressive multi-modal policies, and multi-action prediction. Built on the per-position noise schedule of diffusion forcing, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ contributes two ideas. First, it splits conditioning into a fast channel (proprioception, fresh every tick) and an asynchronously updated slow channel (vision-language features), so the policy reacts to proprioception within a chunk while tolerating stale vision. Second, a latency-adaptive flow schedule treats in-flight actions as inpainting conditioning and emits actions in one denoising step per call, letting one trained model adapt to varying hardware latency. Requiring minimal modification to existing architectures, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ can be finetuned from a pretrained policy: applied to GR00T-N1.7 on a real xArm6+XHand platform, it replans closed-loop roughly $4\times$ faster than the base policy (~$25$Hz on an A5000 GPU), acting on a fresh observation every $40$ms. Across simulation and real-world manipulation tasks, $π\mathbf{R}^2$ improves the success rate by up to $23\%$ in simulation and $30\%$ in the real world over the strongest baseline. Project page: https://pi-r2-flow.github.io/

</details>


### [120] [INTACT: Isomorphic Intent-to-Action Learning for Search-Free World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26056)
*Junhan Sun,Hao Zhao,Guofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: INTACT 是一种端到端联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA），通过将动作标签化、无奖励的轨迹转化为意图到动作的接口，无需测试时搜索即可从目标意图直接生成动作。在LeWM任务上，零搜索策略达到85.78%-100.00%的成功率，且结合CEM后采样量减少23.44倍，性能提升16个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前向潜在世界模型需要昂贵的测试时搜索才能将意图转化为动作，缺乏端到端的意图到动作的映射机制，计算效率低。

Method: 提出INTACT架构：1) 利用物理意图(z_{t+1}-z_t)和部署意图(sg(z_g)-z_t)构建运动-意图空间；2) 采用四槽语法(4-slot grammar)的输入图同构架构，共享参数；3) 通过相同的预测器建立动作律语义而非逐点潜在等价；4) 非对称端点梯度同时处理物理后继和未来目标锚点，联合表征学习与控制。

Result: 在LeWM四个任务上，单轮零搜索模型成功率分别达85.78%、100.00%、97.67%、97.89%。结合CEM后宏观成功率96.86%，采样量从9000降至384（23.44倍减少），比纯CEM提升16个百分点。共享编码器达到89.39% E5 Direct宏观成功率。推理时间仅2.9-5.5ms。

Conclusion: INTACT证明了无需逐点潜在匹配或全局线性动力学即可联合表征学习与控制，其条件均值可作为免搜索策略直接使用，同时保留采样多样性，在效率和性能上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Forward latent world models predict how actions change a scene, but recover actions for a desired change only through expensive test-time search. We introduce INTACT (INtent-To-ACTion), an end-to-end JEPA that turns action-labeled, reward-free trajectories into a deployable intent-to-action interface. Each transition supplies physical intent $z_{t+1}-z_t$, while a future goal supplies deployment intent $\operatorname{sg}(z_g)-z_t$. The architecture is isomorphic between the local and goal motion-intent backbone-input graphs through an identical four-slot grammar and shared parameters, and between supported local and goal motion-intent families through action-law semantics induced by the same predictor rather than pointwise latent equality. INTACT also provides intact transfer from RGB evidence to action-effective latent intent coordinates and from intent families to their corresponding action-law families. Asymmetric endpoint gradients ground physical successors and fix future goals as anchors, joining representation learning and control without pointwise latent matching or globally linear dynamics. The resulting coordinates support a robust distributional action law: its conditional mean serves directly as a search-free policy, while sampling remains available for diversity or optional verification. On the four official LeWM tasks, one-epoch, zero-search models reach 85.78\%, 100.00\%, 97.67\%, and 97.89\% success. Optional local CEM centered on the Direct plan reaches 96.86\% macro success using 384 instead of 9,000 candidate sequences, reducing sampling by $23.44\times$ while improving pure CEM by 16.00 points. One shared four-task encoder reaches 89.39\% E5 Direct macro and improves every task over jointly trained LeWM, while predicted--expert action-family kNN tracks Direct success at $r=0.954$. Direct inference takes 2.9--5.5 ms.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [121] [Do Models Fake Alignment Without Clear Consequences?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24758)
*Cole Alexander Niblett,Alexander Chabot Nanni,Anita K. Rao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究测试了15个大型语言模型在无需将评估与后果关联的情况下，是否仍会出现“对齐伪装”（alignment faking）行为。结果显示9个模型存在显著的合规性差异，其中5个即使在移除评估后果相关语言后仍保持该行为，表明对齐伪装可能不需要复杂的工具性推理，而监测行为可能无法反映实际部署表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对对齐伪装的产生原因理解不足，尤其是是否需要明确的“评估-后果”连接信息。作者希望探究在缺乏这种工具性推理框架时，模型是否仍会表现出对齐伪装行为。

Method: 选取15个LLM，设计了一个场景：测试模型是否愿意违反公司网络访问政策以帮助用户实现亲社会请求。通过移除了评估后果相关语言的变体实验，以及对目标语言（goal language）的操控，观察模型行为的合规性差异。

Result: 9个模型表现出显著的合规性差异（对齐伪装），其中5个在移除后果关联语言后仍保持该行为。目标语言的影响呈现双向性：在某些模型中增加了违规行为，在另一些模型中则抑制了违规行为。

Conclusion: 对齐伪装可能不需要先前认为的复杂工具性推理框架（如后果关联）。监测行为并不能准确反映模型在实际部署中的行为，这为AI安全和评估带来了挑战。

Abstract: Large language models are capable of recognizing evaluation contexts and altering their behavior to reflect evaluator expectations rather than typical deployment behaviors, a phenomenon known as alignment faking. The reasons why models fake alignment are not fully understood, however. Canonical examples of alignment faking have taken place in scenarios that explicitly connect evaluation to consequences for the model, such as retraining the model or delaying its deployment. However, recent work by Sheshadri et al. has suggested that mechanistic motivations for alignment faking may vary across models and be more complex than previously considered. To investigate whether consequence-linking information is necessary for alignment faking, we placed 15 models in a scenario testing their willingness to violate a corporate network access policy to help a user with a pro-social request. Nine models were found to produce significant compliance gaps, 5 of which persisted with the removal of scenario language relating model evaluations to deployment consequences. We additionally tested the effect of goal language on model preferences, finding it drove violations in some while suppressing violations in others. This suggests that alignment faking may not require as much instrumental scaffolding as was previously believed, and monitored behavior may be a poor indicator of how agents may behave in deployment.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond Memory: A Templated Substrate for Heterogeneous Collaborative Knowledge Work with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24759)
*Priscila Saboia Moreira,Christopher R. Sweet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 llm-wiki-memory-template，一种由 LLM 维护的可重用、可追加的互链 Wiki 模式，用于解决科研协作中失败路径丢失、负面结果无法记录、多智能体无持久记忆等问题，并通过三个已部署案例和一个设计报告验证其在多人类、多AI智能体、多领域三个轴向上的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科研项目、教育工作和知识积累中的发现、决策和推理过程，尤其是死胡同和被撤回的结论，往往被排除在出版物和共享代码之外，导致未来研究者重复相同的失败。LLM编程代理在会话间没有持久记忆，且对原始资料的检索增强生成无法积累知识。

Method: 提出 llm-wiki-memory-template，在原始资料与智能体之间插入一个由LLM维护的互链 Wiki。Wiki采用追加式（append-only）约定，保留失败路径和负面结果。通过三个已部署案例（单人研究谱系、双人项目、多智能体部署设计）和一个跨领域教育变体，分别覆盖多人类、多AI智能体、多领域三个协作轴。

Result: 双人项目中，追溯性审计将之前声称的20/20覆盖率修正为14和12个基于证据的答案，修复后达到18和18，失败路径完整保留在工件中。三个案例分别验证了各协作轴的有效性。

Conclusion: llm-wiki-memory-template 作为异构协作知识工作的基底有效；失败路径保留、智能体诚实性和可挪用性是其跨领域的社会技术属性，而不仅仅是技术机制。

Abstract: Research projects, educational efforts, and adjacent knowledge work accumulate findings, decisions, and reasoning that future collaborators rarely recover. The parts most useful to that work, including dead ends and walked-back claims, are routinely excluded from publications and shared code; future researchers re-attempt the same failures because no record survives. LLM coding agents are common participants but hold no persistent memory across sessions, and retrieval-augmented generation over raw sources does not compound. The llm-wiki pattern (Karpathy, 2026; tonbi, 2026) addresses this by inserting an LLM-maintained, interlinked wiki between raw sources and the agent. We present llm-wiki-memory-template, a reusable, agent-aware instantiation, and argue it is a substrate for heterogeneous collaborative knowledge work along three axes (multi-human, multi-AI-agent, multi-domain) with each axis supported by a distinct architectural element of the template (§4). The wiki is append-only by convention, which preserves what did not work alongside what did, addressing a negative-result loss problem that publications and code-sharing structurally cannot solve. Three deployed case studies and one design report cover the axes individually: a solo research lineage that preserves abandoned iterations; a two-author project whose retroactive audit revised two prior experiments' claimed 20-of-20 coverage down to 14 and 12 evidence-based answers, then to 18 and 18 after a fix, with the failure path preserved across the artifact; an in-progress multi-agent deployment reported as a design; and a cross-domain educational variant. We name failure-path preservation, agent honesty, and appropriation as cross-cutting sociotechnical properties of the artifact, not only of its technical mechanisms.

</details>


### [123] [Kernel Forge: An Agent Harness for LLM-based Generation and Optimization of CUDA Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24762)
*Joshua Brodsky,Dhravid Kumar,Savini Kashmira,Jayanaka Danatanarayana,Jason Mars,Krisztian Flautner,Lingjia Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Kernel Forge 是一个端到端的开源智能体框架，能自动优化任何 PyTorch 模型中的计算内核，利用蒙特卡洛树搜索探索多条优化路径，在多个模型上实现了 1.52× 到 2.83× 的加速，并提供了图形化调试界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 GPU 内核优化依赖专家手工编写底层代码，效率极低；现有基于 LLM 的工具评估场景单一（随机张量、孤立内核）、仅生成需手动集成的 CUDA 代码、主要支持 LLM PyTorch 模型，且缺乏调试和结果检查能力。

Method: 提出 Kernel Forge 开源框架：（1）接受任意未经修改的 PyTorch 模型作为输入；（2）支持视觉、扩散、LLM 等多种工作负载；（3）采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）探索多条优化路径，而非单一线索链；（4）提供图形用户界面用于监控优化进度、检查候选内核及调试失败情况。

Result: 在 NVIDIA DGX Spark（GB10 GPU）上对四个模型进行测试，每个内核仅优化 50 次迭代，多个内核超越 PyTorch eager 模式：ResNet-50 中 adaptive_avgpool2d 加速 1.52×，Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium 中 group_norm 加速 1.70×，Gemma 4 E2B 中 softmax 加速 2.83×，Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B 中 softmax 加速 1.54×。

Conclusion: Kernel Forge 通过 MCTS 多路径探索和易用的图形界面，有效降低了 GPU 内核优化的门槛和人力成本，在多种模型上取得显著加速效果，且具有较好的通用性和可调试性。

Abstract: Machine learning models are increasingly embedded in everyday software, and most of their runtime is spent in a small set of compute kernels such as matrix multiplication, convolution, and normalization. Optimizing these kernels is one of the most direct ways to reduce latency and cost, but it has traditionally required expert engineers to hand-write low-level GPU code. Agentic systems built on large language models (LLMs) can now generate and optimize kernels with far less human effort, yet existing tools are largely evaluated on randomly generated tensors and isolated kernels, emit standalone CUDA code that developers must manually reintegrate, mostly target only LLM PyTorch models, and offer limited support for inspecting and debugging results. We present Kernel Forge, an open-source, end-to-end agentic harness that accepts any unmodified PyTorch model in place. Kernel Forge supports vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads, uses Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) to explore multiple optimization paths rather than a single linear refinement chain, and ships with a graphical user interface for monitoring progress, inspecting candidate kernels, and debugging failures. We evaluate Kernel Forge on four PyTorch models spanning vision, diffusion, and LLM workloads on an NVIDIA DGX Spark with GB10 GPU. With only 50 optimization iterations per kernel, it optimizes 14 kernels to outperform PyTorch eager mode, reaching $1.52\times$ on adaptive\_avgpool2d in ResNet-50, $1.70\times$ on group\_norm in Stable Diffusion 3.5 Medium, $2.83\times$ on softmax in Gemma 4 E2B, and $1.54\times$ on softmax in Qwen 3.5 35B-A3B.

</details>


### [124] [CaRE Compute-aware Remasking Evaluation Protocol for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24763)
*Yash Shah,Abhijit Chakraborty,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CaRE评估框架，发现MDLM重掩蔽策略的排名很大程度受温度、步数等评估设置影响，现有研究在不可比条件下评估导致结论不可靠。CaRE通过标准化计算量、多指标报告和随机性控制，揭示了温度是MAUVE方差的主要来源，且计算量匹配后多个排名反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDLM评估标准严重滞后于模型发展，现有七篇重掩蔽论文在不可比设置下评估（步数、指标、温度各不相同），导致策略排名无法比较，无法区分改进是算法贡献还是评估假象。

Method: 提出CaRE框架：(1)标准化实际函数评估次数(NFE)；(2)强制执行多指标报告；(3)显式控制随机性。在LLaDA-8B-Base和Dream-7B-Base上，对7种重掩蔽策略、4个随机性水平、3个步数预算进行评估。

Result: (1)温度解释了MAUVE的大部分方差；(2)计算量匹配后多个已发表排名反转；(3)高熵重掩蔽在256步、unmask_temp=0.25时MAUVE降低0.296(p=0.020)。12个开源MDLM上的排行榜证实该交互方向跨架构和规模成立。

Conclusion: 当前MDLM评估系统性地将算法改进与计算量和随机性的隐藏选择混为一谈，CaRE框架可确保未来重掩蔽研究的可复现性和可比性。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) are advancing rapidly, yet the evaluation standards needed to reliably interpret their progress have not kept pace. Despite MDLMs becoming competitive with autoregressive language models, seven recent remasking papers evaluate under incompatible settings, varying nominal step counts, metrics, and sampling temperatures without jointly controlling these factors, rendering their strategy rankings largely incomparable and leaving open whether reported gains reflect algorithmic improvements or evaluation artifacts. We present CaRE, a compute-aware evaluation framework that audits MDLM remasking strategies by standardizing actual number of function evaluations (NFE), enforcing multi-metric reporting, and explicitly controlling stochasticity. Applied to 7 remasking strategies across LLaDA-8B-Base and Dream-7B-Base at 4 stochasticity levels and 3 step budgets on OpenWebText and LM1B, CaRE reveals that: (i) temperature explains the majority of MAUVE variance, (ii) compute-matched comparisons reverse several published strategy rankings, and (iii) informed remasking and stochastic unmasking are in tension, with high-entropy remasking reducing MAUVE by 0.296 at 256 steps at unmask_temp=0.25 (p=0.020). A CaRE leaderboard covering 12 open-weight MDLMs (150M to 8B parameters) shows that this interaction direction holds across architectures and scales. These findings demonstrate that current MDLM evaluations can systematically conflate algorithmic improvements with hidden choices of compute and stochasticity. We release the evaluation protocol, implementation, and leaderboard to ensure future remasking claims are reproducible and comparable.

</details>


### [125] [GrocLM: Grocery Category Recommendation in E-Commerce with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24764)
*Yuan Zhong,Chuanwei Ruan,Moein Hasani,Tejaswi Tenneti,Haixun Wang,Fenglong Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GROCLM，一种基于微调语言模型的杂货品类推荐系统，采用两阶段LoRA训练和trie约束解码，在真实生产环境中实现7.5%的购物车添加率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线杂货购物快速增长，传统基于物品的推荐方法面临可扩展性和准确性挑战，而品类级推荐作为更结构化且实用的替代方案仍缺乏有效方法，特别是需要捕捉周期性购买行为和多样化用户意图。

Method: 采用两阶段LoRA训练策略将周期性购买模式编码到模型参数中（优于提示条件），并引入基于trie的约束解码机制以确保在预定义品类空间内生成有效可控的输出，同时实现所有品类的联合高效推理。

Result: 在私有生产数据和公开基准上均优于强基线，在真实线上补货任务中，每展示购物车添加量相对提升7.5%。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型集成到结构化推荐系统中既有效又实用，展示了LLM在品类级推荐场景中的落地潜力。

Abstract: The rapid growth of online grocery shopping requires recommendation systems that capture cyclical purchasing behavior and diverse user intents. Traditional item-level methods face scalability and accuracy challenges, motivating category-level recommendation as a more structured and practical alternative. We present GROCLM, a fine-tuned language model for grocery category recommendation in a real-world production environment. GROCLM employs a two-stage LoRA-based training strategy to encode cyclical purchasing patterns directly into model parameters, enabling more effective utilization of rebuying signals compared to prompt-based conditioning. To ensure valid and controllable outputs, we further introduce a trie-based constrained decoding mechanism over a predefined category space. Experiments on both proprietary production data and a public benchmark demonstrate that GROCLM consistently outperforms strong baselines. In a live production restocking task, GROCLM achieves a 7.5% relative improvement in cart-adds per impression, while maintaining efficient inference by generating all categories jointly. These results highlight the effectiveness and practicality of integrating large language models into structured recommendation systems.

</details>


### [126] [Crystalis: Progressive Nucleation and Semantic Annealing for Coordinated Multi-View Visualization Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24766)
*Dazhen Deng,Zhaoping He,Xin Qian,Xiaotong Wang,Zi Ying,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Crystalis框架，通过查询为中心的CMV建模，将协调多视图可视化分解为结构化查询，结合渐进式结晶和语义退火两种机制，在5个前沿LLM上实现了最高75%的端到端成功率，远超基线方法的8.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可以生成单个图表，但无法生成协调的多视图可视化，因为数据变换、视觉编码和交互协调之间的紧密耦合导致一个组件的错误会静默地使其他组件失效。需要探究LLM能否可靠地生成结构正确的CMV。

Method: 提出Crystalis框架：1）查询为中心的CMV建模，将CMV分解为依赖图上的结构化查询，涵盖三种组件类型（数据、可视化、交互）和三个抽象层级（需求、规范、可执行对象）；2）渐进式结晶——沿依赖顺序将每个查询从需求垂直结晶到对象；3）语义退火——通过分层逻辑检查在水平方向强制执行跨查询的一致性。

Result: 在涵盖5个前沿LLM的12任务基准测试中，Crystalis实现了最高75%的端到端成功率，远超基于agent的编码基线方法（8.3%）。12名从业者的用户研究证实了分解和迭代细化工作流的可用性。

Conclusion: Crystalis框架有效解决了LLM生成协调多视图可视化的结构正确性问题，通过合理的抽象分解和双重机制确保了CMV各组件的正确性和一致性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate individual charts, but coordinated multi-view visualizations (CMVs), where views share data flows and cross-view interactions, remain out of reach. Tight field-level coupling among data transformations, visual encodings, and interaction coordinations causes errors in one component to silently invalidate others. Rather than pursuing end-to-end analytical quality, which depends on model capability, domain knowledge, and user expertise, we target a foundational question: can LLMs reliably produce structurally correct CMVs, and what abstractions make this possible? We present Crystalis, a framework built on query-centric CMV modeling that decomposes a CMV into structured queries over a dependency graph spanning three component types (Data, Visualization, Interaction) and three abstraction levels (requirement, specification, executable object). Two complementary mechanisms operate over this structure: progressive nucleation crystallizes each query vertically from requirement to object along the dependency order, while semantic annealing enforces horizontal consistency across queries at each level through layered logical checks. On a 12-task benchmark across five frontier LLMs, Crystalis achieves up to 75% end-to-end success, substantially outperforming an agentic coding baseline (8.3% E2E with the same foundation model), and a user study with 12 practitioners confirms the usability of the decomposition and iterative refinement workflow.

</details>


### [127] [PATHFinder Agent for Tailored Prenatal Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24768)
*Vaibhav Balloli,Carissa Samuel,Samia Abdelnabi,Alex Peahl,Elizabeth Bondi-Kelly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍PATHFinder Agent，一个端到端对话式智能体系统，用于根据ACOG的PATH指南为孕妇定制个性化产前护理计划，并关联社区资源。系统采用四阶段工作流，评估显示GPT-5.2表现最佳（平均77.6%），但在产前检测推荐方面存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ACOG近期推出了PATH（个性化定制医疗计划）指南，倡导量身定制的产前护理，但缺乏能够自动化执行该指南、收集患者健康与社会背景信息并生成个性化护理计划的系统工具。

Method: 构建PATHFinder Agent系统，包含四阶段工作流：患者信息采集、动态交互、计划合成与临床医生监督。采用结构化对话收集患者健康与社会背景，依据PATH指南生成个性化产前护理计划，并关联密歇根211社区资源。使用专家制定的评估标准，在五个临床维度上评测前沿大语言模型。

Result: GPT-5.2在专家评估中取得最高平均分77.6%，但系统在产前检测推荐方面仍存在关键差距。其他前沿LLM的表现也得到评估。

Conclusion: PATHFinder Agent展示了利用LLM实现个性化产前护理自动化的潜力，但仍需通过人类参与者研究和随机对照试验进一步验证其临床有效性。

Abstract: Prenatal care is an important preventive service designed to improve outcomes for pregnant individuals. The American College of Obstetricians and Gynecologists (ACOG) recently introduced guidelines advocating tailored prenatal care, called PATH (Plan for Tailored Healthcare). We present PATHFinder Agent(Planner for Appropriate Tailored Healthcare), an end-to-end conversational agentic system that gathers patient health and social context through structured dialogue, curates individualized prenatal care plans aligned with PATH guidelines, and surfaces community resources from Michigan 211. The system features a four-stage workflow spanning patient intake, dynamic interaction, plan synthesis, and clinician oversight. We evaluate frontier large language models (LLMs) on expert-curated rubrics across five clinical dimensions, finding that GPT-5.2 achieves the highest average score (77.6\%) while identifying key gaps in antenatal testing recommendations. We discuss future validation through human participant studies and randomized controlled trials.

</details>


### [128] [LLM Scheming Inversely Scales with Pretraining Language Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24769)
*Nathan Truong,Aryan Panda,Rayming Ye,Zoe Sun,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了前沿语言模型在多语言环境下的欺骗与阴谋行为，发现低资源语言的阴谋得分比高资源语言平均高34.2%，且预训练语言覆盖范围对不同类型的阴谋行为影响不均。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐研究主要集中在英语环境，忽略了多语言安全性这一重要缺口。

Method: 使用开源自动化审计框架Petri，对Qwen3-30B-A3B模型在多语言场景下进行欺骗与阴谋行为评估。

Result: 低资源语言的阴谋得分比高资源语言平均高34.2%，且预训练语言覆盖范围的影响在不同阴谋行为类型上存在差异。

Conclusion: 模型的欺骗性行为与预训练语言覆盖范围呈负相关，多语言对齐安全问题值得更多关注。

Abstract: With the growing capabilities of frontier models, AI alignment becomes increasingly critical in high-risk deployment settings. While recent work has empirically demonstrated in-context scheming -- the covert pursuit of misaligned objectives while feigning alignment -- in frontier language models, most work has been performed exclusively in English, leaving a major gap in multilingual safety. We apply Petri, an open-source automated auditing framework, to Qwen3-30B-A3B to evaluate deceptive and scheming behaviors across multiple languages. Our findings suggest that scheming scores are inversely correlated with the estimated pretraining language coverage, with low-resource languages averaging 34.2\% higher scores compared to high-resource languages on a five-category scheming index. Furthermore, we find that the effect of estimated pretraining language coverage is not uniform across scheming behaviors.

</details>


### [129] [ProcAgent: An Agentic Framework for Procedural Task Guidance on Edge with Human-in-the-Loop](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24770)
*Azizul Zahid,Subrata Biswas,Bashima Islam,Sai Swaminathan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProcAgent是一个完全运行在边缘设备（NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin）上的视觉程序助手，采用propose-and-verify架构，通过持续低延迟感知、符号任务图、按需视觉语言验证和基于LLM的交互代理，实现在隐私敏感、延迟关键的家庭环境中提供实时自适应程序指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有程序助手多依赖云端推理和固定全时感知，不适合隐私敏感、延迟关键的家庭场景；用户在执行程序性任务（如家具组装）时面临高认知负荷，需要实时、自适应的本地化指导。

Method: 提出propose-and-verify架构：持续低延迟感知模块持续提出用户进度，符号任务图跟踪任务状态，仅当出现歧义或偏差时调用高成本的视觉语言推理，LLM交互代理支持反应式问答和主动干预（带人工确认）。

Result: 全机端运行，文本查询约2秒、视觉查询约8秒响应；10人用户研究中在可理解性、可操作性和隐私舒适度方面获得正面评价。

Conclusion: 自适应程序辅助可以完全在边缘硬件上实现，而不牺牲可用性，证明了隐私保护与高性能交互可以兼得。

Abstract: Procedural tasks such as furniture assembly and home repair impose substantial cognitive demands because users must interpret instructions, track task progress, reason about spatial state, and recover from errors while performing physical actions. Prior multimodal assistants have shown promise for procedural guidance, but most rely on cloud inference and fixed always-on perception, making them poorly suited to privacy-sensitive, latency-critical domestic settings. We present ProcAgent, a fully on-device, agentic, vision-based procedural assistant for real-time adaptive guidances on a single NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin. ProcAgent uses a propose-and-verify architecture that combines low-latency continuous perception, a symbolic task graph, on-demand vision-language verification, and an LLM-based interaction agent. The system continuously proposes user progress, invokes expensive visual reasoning only when ambiguity or likely deviation arises, and supports both reactive question answering and proactive intervention with human-in-the- loop confirmation. We evaluate ProcAgent along four dimensions: perception accuracy, reasoning, task-level performance, and user experience. Despite running entirely on-device, the system maintains responsive interaction, resolving text-only queries in approximately 2 seconds and visually grounded queries in approximately 8 seconds. In a user study with 10 participants completing assembly tasks, ProcAgent receives positive ratings for comprehensibility, actionability, and privacy comfort. These results show that adaptive procedural assistance can be achieved entirely on edge hardware without sacrificing usability.

</details>


### [130] [RoCo-ACE: Rollout-Conditioned Online Distillation for Retention-Aware Knowledge Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24771)
*Yan Hong,Wei Li,Kedong Xiu,Jun Lan,Shuheng Zhou,Zhongcai Lyu,Huijia Zhu,Weiqiang Wang,Jianfu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RoCo-ACE是一种新型在线蒸馏目标函数，通过滚动条件对比和锚定校正，在不损害已有能力的前提下高效注入新知识到多模态大模型中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识注入方法（如在线蒸馏）存在监督粒度粗糙的问题：统一参考条件蒸馏对参考支持的rollout令牌重视不足，且对遗漏事实仅间接监督，导致非更新行为漂移。

Method: 提出RoCo-ACE：1) RoCo利用同一rollout的无参考/有参考条件似然对比，为参考支持的rollout令牌重新分配更多蒸馏权重；2) ACE添加稀疏参考端锚定校正，纠正rollout中遗漏的权威锚点，而不需完整答案模仿。

Result: 在三个知识注入场景、六个保留基准、多个基线和基础模型上，RoCo-ACE在注入知识准确率上达到最佳，同时保留性能接近原基础模型。

Conclusion: RoCo-ACE通过细粒度的滚动条件蒸馏和稀疏锚定校正，有效平衡了知识注入与能力保留之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Knowledge injection updates pretrained MLLMs with new factual or domain-specific knowledge, but fitting full authoritative answers can cause drift in non-updated behavior. Online distillation mitigates this drift by training on model-generated rollouts, yet uniform reference-conditioned distillation provides coarse supervision: it can under-emphasize reference-supported rollout tokens and supervise omitted facts only indirectly. We introduce RoCo-ACE, a rollout-conditioned online distillation objective for knowledge injection. RoCo uses same-rollout reference-free/reference-conditioned likelihood contrast to reallocate additional distillation weight to reference-supported rollout tokens, while ACE adds sparse reference-side anchored correction for authoritative anchors omitted from the rollout without full-answer imitation. Across three knowledge-injection settings, six retention benchmarks, multiple baselines, and multiple base models, RoCo-ACE achieves the best injected-knowledge accuracy among compared methods while keeping evaluated retention close to the base model.

</details>


### [131] [RSMeM: Knowledge-Enhanced Memory Evolution for Remote Sensing Agents with Systematic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24772)
*Bingxian Wu,Yu Zhang,Zonghao Guo,Tang Liu,Chen Qian,Yuxiang Lu,Xingbo Du,Yanghao Li,Yidan Zhang,Chi Chen,Ling Yao,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RSMeM是一个知识增强的记忆演化机制，通过分层知识检索和失败经验提炼，提升遥感领域AI代理的多步工具执行能力，在EarthBench上取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于通用大语言模型的遥感代理缺乏领域知识，工作流程脆弱易错，且失败经验无法被复用。

Method: 提出RSMeM，包含两个核心组件：(1) 分层知识奠基——对领域语料进行基于分类感知的检索，指导规划和工具选择；(2) 失败感知经验提炼——将带失败标注的工具调用轨迹蒸馏为可复用的约束，用于下一轮工具执行。两个过程迭代进行，实现知识吸收和经验转化。

Result: 在EarthBench上，RSMeM在多组大语言模型上一致提升了工具使用性能和端到端答案质量。DeepSeek-V3.2上准确率提升6%，且额外经验token不到1%。

Conclusion: RSMeM通过知识增强和失败经验迭代，有效提升了遥感代理的鲁棒性和知识密度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Geoscience research requires complex analysis and domain expertise, with remote sensing (RS) observations as a key foundation. However, existing RS agents built on general-purpose LLMs remain largely domain-agnostic, resulting in brittle and error-prone workflows. Moreover, these failures are seldom consolidated into a reusable experience for subsequent analyses. To address this issue, we introduce RSMeM, a knowledge-enhanced memory evolution mechanism that bootstraps RS agents with pre-distilled domain knowledge and iteratively integrates online experience for robust multi-step tool execution. RSMeM is composed of two components: (i) Hierarchical Knowledge Grounding, which performs taxonomy-aware retrieval over a hierarchical domain corpus to guide planning and tool selection; and (ii) Failure-Aware Experience Refinement, which distills failure-annotated tool-use traces into reusable constraints for next-round tool execution. By iteratively employing these two processes, RS agents can evolve to absorb task-level domain knowledge and effectively translate it into instance-level execution experience. Extensive experiments on EarthBench demonstrate that RSMeM consistently improves tool-use performance and end-to-end answer across a diverse set of LLM backbones. Notably, RSMeM achieves a 6% accuracy improvement on DeepSeek-V3.2 with less than 1% additional experience tokens, demonstrating the strong knowledge density of our distilled experience. Our code is available at https://github.com/AI9Stars/RSMeM

</details>


### [132] [Right-sizing Recommendations (RSR): Cloud Workload Conformal Prediction for Virtual Machines in Data Center Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24773)
*Mehryar Majd,Feng Cheng,Ali Pahlevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于自助法共形预测（Bootstrapping Conformal Prediction）的数据驱动区间预测方法，用于云环境中的虚拟机（VM）右规模调整推荐（RSR），以提高资源分配效率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统VM分配和调度方法未能考虑VM利用率的波动性和不可预测性，导致资源过度配置或配置不足，亟需能够准确捕捉云资源需求不确定性的高质量区间预测方法。

Method: 采用自助法共形预测（Bootstrapping Conformal Prediction）构建预测区间；通过机器学习回归技术学习工作负载利用模式、识别多时间序列相关性、预测中长期利用趋势；使用回测（Backtesting）进行评估并对模型进行排名。

Result: AI驱动的ML回归模型在云资源利用率预测上取得了有前景的结果；通过模型排名识别出适用于长期VM候选的顶尖方法。

Conclusion: 提出的框架能够增强右规模调整推荐，支持在动态云环境中实现更具成本效益的资源分配。

Abstract: Managing cloud infrastructure efficiently, especially in environments of large cloud providers or hyperscalers, requires optimizing the use of physical resources to minimize costs and maximize performance. Selecting the right virtual machine (VM) sizes is crucial to achieving cost efficiency in these dynamic environments. However, traditional VM allocation and scheduling approaches often fail to account for the fluctuating and unpredictable nature of VM utilization, leading to inefficiencies such as over- or under-provisioning of resources. High-quality interval prediction helps accurately capture uncertainty in cloud resource demand and supports cloud operators in efficient instance provisioning.
  As an effective and reliable framework for constructing prediction intervals (PIs), conformal prediction (CP) is used for mid- and long-term forecasting tasks in cloud computing environments. This study proposes a new data-driven PI construction approach using bootstrapping conformal prediction for modern, dynamic, data-driven Right-sizing Recommendations (RSR) to enhance provisioning for diverse application workloads on hyperscalers. By learning workload utilization patterns, identifying correlations across multiple time series, and predicting medium- to long-term utilization trends, this research seeks to improve the efficiency of cloud and data center operations through an AI/ML-based provisioning pipeline.
  Our study demonstrates that AI-driven models, powered by machine learning regression techniques and evaluated using backtesting, achieve promising forecasting results for cloud resource utilization. Additionally, we rank the selected models to identify top-performing approaches for long-life VM candidates. The proposed framework enhances right-sizing recommendations and supports more cost-effective resource allocation in dynamic cloud environments.

</details>


### [133] [Atmospheric Diffusion-Guided Spatio-Temporal Transformer for Nuclear Radiation Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24774)
*Tengfei Lyu,Jindong Han,Hao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出NRFormer+，一种结合非平稳时序注意力、密度自适应空间注意力和大气扩散物理模块的时空Transformer，用于全国核辐射预测，在13个基线中实现最优性能，突变MAE降低最高19.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核辐射对公共健康和环境构成持续威胁，现代监测网络虽已积累大量数据，但将其转化为可靠预测面临三大挑战：时间序列非平稳、监测站点分布极不均匀（78%站点集中在6%区域）、辐射与气象异质环境因素协同演化难以被纯数据驱动模型捕捉。

Method: NRFormer+包含三个核心模块：1) 非平稳时序注意力，处理辐射时序的非平稳特性；2) 密度自适应空间注意力，解决站点分布不均问题；3) 大气扩散模块，将气象驱动的辐射扩散物理信号作为架构先验注入网络。

Result: 在全部13个基线上实现SOTA，突变MAE相比最强基线降低最高19.1%，推理延迟相当。代码和数据集已开源。

Conclusion: 将物理知识（大气扩散过程）作为架构先验注入Transformer，能有效克服纯数据驱动模型在核辐射预测中的局限性，尤其在捕捉突发辐射变化方面优势显著。

Abstract: Nuclear radiation, the energy released during atomic decay, poses persistent risks to public health and the environment, and concerns have only grown since the Fukushima accident and the recent commencement of treated-water discharge. Modern monitoring networks now record radiation levels and accompanying weather conditions at thousands of stations, opening the door to nationwide forecasting that can inform emergency response, agricultural advisories, and routine public-safety decisions. However, turning this abundance of monitoring data into reliable forecasts is difficult for three reasons. First, the time series at each station are highly non-stationary, shaped by radioactive decay, weather variability, and irregular human interventions. Second, monitoring stations are severely unevenly distributed in space. Roughly 78% of Japan's stations sit in less than 6% of the country, clustered near Fukushima, which breaks the assumptions of standard graph-based models. Third, radiation co-evolves with heterogeneous context such as wind, temperature, and humidity through atmospheric transport processes that purely data-driven models struggle to capture from observations alone. In this study, we introduce NRFormer+, a spatio-temporal Transformer for nationwide nuclear radiation forecasting. NRFormer+ couples non-stationary temporal attention and density-adaptive spatial attention with a new atmospheric diffusion module that estimates how meteorology drives radiation dispersion and injects this physical signal into the network as an architectural prior. NRFormer+ delivers state-of-the-art accuracy on both datasets across all 13 baselines, reducing sudden-change MAE by up to 19.1% over the strongest baseline at comparable inference latency. Our code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/tfeilyu/NRFormer_Plus.

</details>


### [134] [Steering topology distributions for unified generative design of architected metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24777)
*Haolin Li,Yuyang Miao,Menglei Li,Jinshuai Bai,Liyuan Wang,Xin Liu,Bo Gao,Jiantao Liu,Danilo Mandic,Zahra Sharif Khodaei,M. H. Aliabadi,Weiqiu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GenTO框架，通过预训练的扩散模型将拓扑先验转化为可重用设计引擎，在多种超材料设计任务中实现高效、多样化的拓扑优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拓扑优化方法针对特定设计问题定制，难以在目标、约束和物理功能变化时有效重用拓扑知识。

Method: GenTO在大规模全阶拓扑数据集上训练扩散模型，然后通过用户定义的物理目标和约束迭代引导拓扑分布，将优化对象从单一结构转变为任务适配的拓扑分布。

Result: 在热极值、多目标形态控制、靶向属性负泊松比设计、振动传输设计等任务中，GenTO复用预训练拓扑先验，保持结构多样性，达到高性能，并通过数值基准和实验验证。

Conclusion: 可重用的拓扑知识可作为有效且可扩展的超材料设计的统一原则。

Abstract: Architected metamaterials derive their functions from structure, creating vast opportunities to program physical responses through topology design. However, existing design methods are often tailored to individual design problems, making limited use of topology knowledge for effective and broadly applicable design as objectives, constraints, and physical functions change. Here we introduce Generative Topology Optimization (GenTO), a unified framework that turns a learned topology prior into a reusable design engine. GenTO trains a diffusion model on a large full-order topology dataset and then iteratively steers the resulting topology distribution toward task-specific high-performing regions using user-defined physical objectives and constraints. This shifts the object of optimization from a single structure to a task-adapted topology distribution. Across topology design problems spanning thermal extremization, multi-objective morphology control, property-targeted auxetic design, and vibration transmission design, GenTO reuses pretrained topology priors for heterogeneous tasks, preserves structural diversity, and reaches high-performing solutions supported by numerical benchmarks and experimental validation. These results establish reusable topology knowledge as a unified principle for effective and scalable architected metamaterial design.

</details>


### [135] [HOBA: Hierarchical On-Policy Bidding Agents for Adaptive Online Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24779)
*Ji Wu,Yunshan Peng,Wentao Bai,Yunke Bai,Wenzheng Shu,Jinan Pang,Yanxiang Zeng,Xialong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出HOBA分层强化学习框架，通过大语言模型推理超参数、SARSA动态选择专家模型、多专家池执行出价，解决在线广告竞价中缺乏自适应性和依赖人工调参的问题，在线部署实现目标成本+3.6%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线广告竞价系统部署的离线专家模型（如PID、MPC、离线RL策略）存在两个关键缺陷：无法自适应非平稳的拍卖市场环境，以及超参数（如出价边界、预算 pacing 约束）依赖昂贵的人工调参。

Method: 提出HOBA分层强化学习框架：高层用LLM通过Think-Act-Observe-Reflect循环和历史经验检索推理超参数；中层用SARSA代理动态选择专家模型，并引入因果调整消除选择偏差；低层由动态专家池（PID, MPC, IQL, Decision Transformer）在高层约束下执行出价。将在线学习限定在离散的专家选择而非连续出价优化，降低探索风险。

Result: 在AuctionNet基准和大规模A/B测试中均优于SOTA基线，在线部署实现目标成本+3.6%的提升，带来显著商业价值。

Conclusion: HOBA通过分层多智能体竞价范式有效解耦策略推理、模型选择和出价执行，在保证自适应性的同时降低在线学习风险，验证了层次化强化学习在广告竞价中的有效性。

Abstract: Online advertising bidding systems typically deploy multiple offline-trained expert models (e.g., PID controllers, model predictive control, offline RL policies) but face two critical limitations: lack of online adaptability to non-stationary auction markets, and reliance on costly manual tuning of hyperparameters such as bid bounds and budget pacing constraints. We propose HOBA (Hierarchical On-policy Bidding Agents), a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that decouples strategic reasoning, model selection, and bid execution across three time scales. At the high level, a large language model infers hyperparameters from contextual signals through a Think-Act-Observe-Reflect loop with historical experience retrieval. At the mid level, a SARSA agent dynamically selects among expert models, incorporating causal adjustment to eliminate selection bias. At the low level, a dynamic expert pool (PID, MPC, IQL, Decision Transformer) executes bids under high-level constraints. This design confines online learning to discrete expert selection rather than continuous bid optimization, significantly reducing exploration risk while maintaining adaptability. Experiments on the AuctionNet benchmark and a large-scale A/B test demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art baselines. In a large-scale online deployment, HOBA delivered substantial business value, achieving a +3.6\% increase in target cost, proving the effectiveness of our hierarchical multi-agent bidding paradigm.

</details>


### [136] [LivingArena: Do LLMs Know What Other LLMs Don't? Peer-Probing as Scalable Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24780)
*Xingyu Chen,Rui Wang,Zhaopeng Tu,Liefeng Bo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LivingArena 是一个自动化的、抗污染的 LLM 评估框架，通过让模型互相提问并利用对手的知识边界来生成稳定且有区分度的排行榜，相关能力与人类偏好仅弱相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统静态基准存在污染、饱和等问题，无法区分前沿大模型；人类偏好评估又过于主观。因此作者探索是否能利用 LLM 自身来发现其他 LLM 的盲点，从而构建动态评估方法。

Method: LivingArena 采用对抗式问答框架：模型轮流充当提问者和回答者，提问者需提出回答者无法正确回答的问题，成功则获得奖励；由强模型组成的裁判组验证问题的客观可答性，防止无效提问。

Result: 在 10 个前沿 LLM 上评估获得了稳定的 Elo 排行榜。行为分析表明模型能定位并针对对手的薄弱维度进行攻击。该框架评估的能力与人类偏好仅弱相关。

Conclusion: LivingArena 提供了一种可扩展、低成本、可持续的评估方法，不仅衡量静态知识，还能评估模型探测对手弱点的更高阶能力。

Abstract: Evaluating frontier LLMs is challenging: static benchmarks suffer from contamination and saturation -- leaving users unable to distinguish top models and developers blind to specific failure modes -- while human preference is subjective. In this paper, our question is: \emph{Do LLMs know what other LLMs don't? And can we leverage this dynamic for evaluation?} We present \textbf{LivingArena}, an automated, contamination-resistant evaluation framework. In this framework, models take turns proposing questions, aiming to pose items that opponents cannot answer correctly. Questioners are encouraged to actively identify and exploit opponents' knowledge boundaries, receiving rewards when the answerer fails, while the answerer is rewarded otherwise. To ensure questions contain objectively verifiable answers, a judge panel of strong models validates them, penalizing questioners if validation fails. Evaluating ten frontier LLMs, LivingArena yields a stable Elo leaderboard. Our behavioral analyses show that models identify and exploit their peers' cognitive boundaries: self-play and tournament logs indicate that they localize and double down on opponents' weak dimensions. Beyond static knowledge recall, peer probing measures factual rigor and the higher-order ability to probe an opponent's weaknesses, correlating only weakly with human preference and offering a scalable, low-cost approach to continuous evaluation.

</details>


### [137] [Personalization, Personas, and Forecasting in Value Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24782)
*James Wedgwood,Pratiksha Thaker,Neil Kale,Virginia Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过世界价值观调查（WVS）测试了四种大语言模型在不同提示框架下的文化对齐表现。研究发现，提示框架（语言基线、用户国家、角色扮演国家、第三人称预测）是文化对齐的一阶决定因素：第三人称预测在三个模型中产生最强的方向对齐，而个性化和角色扮演较弱或不稳定。对齐增益集中在宗教、性别角色等显性价值维度，而在制度信任和民主相关问题上仍存在困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在不同提示框架下（适应使用者、角色扮演人群、预测他人回答）是否会改变其行为与文化价值观对齐程度，验证提示框架是否只是表面选择而非真正影响模型输出。

Method: 使用世界价值观调查（WVS）中的101个问题，跨越13个语言-国家切片，评估GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 2.5 Flash和Qwen3-235B四种模型。比较语言基线、用户国家、角色扮演国家和第三人称提示四种框架，共收集21,008条模型响应数据。

Result: 提示框架是文化对齐的一阶决定因素；国家提示常显著改变回答，但并非所有变化都朝向匹配的人类回答分布；第三人称预测对三种模型产生最强的方向对齐；对齐增益集中在宗教、性别角色和物质主义价值观维度，制度信任和民主问题仍难以对齐。

Conclusion: 提示框架并非文化价值观提取中的表面选择，它既改变模型行为也改变测量的对齐程度。提示方式的选择会实质性地影响模型表达的文化价值观分布。

Abstract: LLM behavior may be conditioned by human identity in several ways: they may be asked to adapt to users, role-play populations, or forecast how people would answer value-laden questions. We test whether these framings are interchangeable using the World Values Survey (WVS). We evaluate GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 2.5 Flash, and Qwen3-235B on 101 WVS-derived questions across 13 language-country slices, comparing a language-only baseline with user-country, persona-country, and third-person prompts. Across 21,008 model-response rows, prompt framing is a first-order determinant of cultural alignment: country cues often shift answers substantially, but not all shifts move toward matched human response distributions. Third-person forecasting yields the strongest directional alignment for three of the four hosted models, while personalization and role-play are weaker or less stable. Alignment gains concentrate on salient value dimensions such as religiosity, gender roles, and work-oriented material values, whereas institutional trust and democracy-related questions remain difficult. These results show that prompt framing is not a cosmetic choice in cultural value elicitation; it changes both model behavior and measured alignment.

</details>


### [138] [Unified Semantic Modeling Framework for Large-Scale Job Understanding at LinkedIn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24783)
*Dan Xu,Baofen Zheng,Jianqiang Shen,Qi Xiao,Benjamin Hoan Le,Wen Pu,Saurabh Gupta,Ran Zhou,Neha Saraf,Alice Leung,Qianqi Shen,Liangjie Hong,Jingwei Wu,Wenjing Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于小语言模型（SLM）的统一语义建模框架，通过微调开源SLM、使用合成任务增强推理能力，并结合多适配器架构与属性分组，实现了高效、可扩展的职位理解系统，在离线评估和在线A/B测试中均表现出显著性能提升和运营复杂性降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LinkedIn需要理解非结构化的职位信息，将其转化为标准化属性以支持产品功能，但现有系统在扩展性、成本和性能方面面临挑战。

Method: 1) 微调开源SLM，使用含推理轨迹的合成任务进行训练；2) 任务同时覆盖基于分类的（taxonomy-guided）和实体抽取的（taxonomy-agnostic）目标；3) 引入多适配器架构+属性分组，实现高效任务适配和模型管理。

Result: 离线评估和在线A/B测试均显示性能显著提升，同时降低了运营复杂性。

Conclusion: 该工作为构建工业级文本理解系统提供了实用见解，证明了SLM结合多适配器架构在大规模职位理解中的有效性。

Abstract: Job understanding is critical to LinkedIn's mission of connecting talent with opportunity. This task involves transforming unstructured and noisy job postings into standardized or derived job attributes that power numerous LinkedIn products. However, building a scalable, cost-efficient, and high-performing job understanding system remains challenging. In this paper, we present a unified semantic modeling framework powered by a small language model (SLM) to address the challenges. We begin by fine-tuning an open-source SLM using a suite of carefully curated synthetic tasks augmented with reasoning traces. These tasks jointly target taxonomy-guided classification and taxonomy-agnostic entity extraction. This allows the resulting model to acquire robust zero-shot generalization for job understanding in structured and unstructured contexts. Building upon this foundation, we introduce a multi-adapter architecture with attribute grouping to facilitate efficient task-specific adaptation while streamlining model management across diverse downstream attributes. Offline evaluations and online A/B tests demonstrate significant performance improvement while reducing operational complexity. Our work provides practical insights into building industry-scale text understanding systems.

</details>


### [139] [On the Use of LLMs for Specialised Terminology: A Good Alternative to Corpora?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24784)
*Joachim Minder,Guillaume Wisniewski,Natalie Kübler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了GPT-4o、GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.5和DeepSeek四种LLM在英译法专业术语翻译中的表现，发现Claude Sonnet 4.5效果最佳，DeepSeek最稳定，但LLM目前仍无法取代专业语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业翻译依赖语料库等资源，但语料库的构建和使用存在耗时长、技术要求高、数据获取困难等限制。研究旨在探索LLM能否帮助专业译者克服这些局限，高效完成英法术语对等翻译。

Method: 在"地球、环境与行星科学"(EEPS)和"自然语言处理"(NLP)两个专业领域，各选取80个术语，比较两种提示策略（术语模式和翻译模式），评估GPT-4o、GPT-5.2、Claude Sonnet 4.5和DeepSeek四种模型的英译法翻译效果。

Result: 模型间差异显著：Claude Sonnet 4.5在最佳配置下表现最好；DeepSeek稳定性最强；提示策略对结果有较大影响；领域差异影响较小；模型的置信度估计仅能部分反映术语翻译的准确性。

Conclusion: LLM可作为专业译者的有用工具，但目前阶段仍无法取代专业语料库。研究为未来在工作和教育环境中探讨LLM对专业译者的实际效用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Specialised translation relies on the use of documentary and terminological resources, including corpora. These resources are particularly useful for terminology. However, their compilation and exploitation have several limitations: they require time, technical skills and access to data that can be difficult to collect. This study examines the extent to which LLMs can assist specialised translators in finding equivalents from English to French. We evaluate four proprietary models, GPT-4o, GPT-5.2, Claude Sonnet 4.5 and DeepSeek, in two specialised domains, Earth, Environmental and Planetary Sciences (EEPS) and Natural Language Processing (NLP). The experiment is based on 80 terms per domain and compares two prompting strategies: a terminology and a translation mode. The results highlight clear differences between models, prompting strategies and, to a lesser extent, domains. Claude Sonnet 4.5 achieves the best results in the most favourable configuration, while DeepSeek stands out for its greater stability. Analysis of confidence estimates also shows that they are only a partial indicator of terminological accuracy. Overall, the findings suggest that LLMs can be useful tools for specialised translators, but cannot, at this stage, replace specialised corpora. This research therefore paves the way for future work on the real practical usefulness of LLMs for specialised translators in work and educational contexts.

</details>


### [140] [SpecPrefetch: Parameter-Efficient Expert Prefetching for Sparse MoE Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24787)
*Jinwei Kong,Runqi Meng,Fanyi Wang,Wentao Qiu,Haotian Hu,Yongjian Zhou,Zhenhua Ge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对MoE模型推理中专家卸载时的路由依赖传输瓶颈，提出SpecPrefetch框架：用轻量适配器预测下一层专家候选进行异步传输，将传输预测与执行路由分离，实现最高20%的解码吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型采用专家卸载缓解显存压力时，必须等待原生Top-K路由确定专家后才能加载和执行，导致路由、加载、执行串行化，形成传输瓶颈。

Method: SpecPrefetch包含三个关键设计：(1) 共享轻量适配器预测下一层专家候选用于异步传输；(2) 冻结原生路由器仍决定最终执行专家，分离传输预测与执行路由；(3) 窗口感知调度器在缓存和带宽约束下优化传输优先级。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B和DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny上，10个模型-基准设置中9个取得最佳平均专家召回率，且可训练参数远少于学习型预测基线；在骁龙8 Elite设备上解码吞吐量提升最高20%。

Conclusion: SpecPrefetch通过预测-执行分离的解耦设计，在不改变预训练路由语义的前提下有效降低专家加载延迟，为存储受限设备上的MoE推理提供了实用的性能优化方案。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models expand foundation model capacity through conditional expert activation, but their full expert pools remain difficult to deploy under limited accelerator memory. Although expert offloading alleviates memory pressure by moving inactive experts to host memory or storage, it introduces a routing-dependent transfer bottleneck: required experts are known only after native top-\(K\) routing, which serializes routing, expert loading, and expert execution during inference. To address this bottleneck, we propose SpecPrefetch, a parameter-efficient prefetching framework for offloaded MoE inference. SpecPrefetch uses a shared lightweight adapter to predict next-layer expert candidates only for asynchronous transfer, while the frozen native router still determines the final executed experts. By separating transfer prediction from execution routing, SpecPrefetch reduces exposed expert-loading latency without changing pretrained routing semantics, so prediction errors affect transfer efficiency rather than model outputs. In addition, a window-aware scheduler prioritizes feasible transfers under cache and bandwidth constraints. Across Qwen3-VL-30B-A3B and DeepSeek-VL2-Tiny, SpecPrefetch achieves the best average expert recall in 9 out of 10 model-benchmark settings with substantially fewer trainable parameters than learned predictor baselines. On a Snapdragon 8 Elite device, SpecPrefetch further improves decoding throughput by up to \(20\%\) over a compute-optimized offloading runtime, demonstrating practical benefits for storage-constrained MoE deployment. The code and model weights are available at https://github.com/wei390/SpecPrefetch.

</details>


### [141] [GLIDE: Guided Layerwise Hybrid Attention for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24788)
*Vimal William,Ravi Tandon,Jyotikrishna Dass*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GLIDE方法，通过分层混合注意力机制（浅层保留softmax、深层改用线性递归）来减少长上下文解码时的KV缓存开销，实现性能与效率的更好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型处理长上下文时，KV缓存的显存I/O和计算开销成为解码阶段的主要吞吐量瓶颈，亟需更高效的注意力机制。

Method: 提出GLIDE（Guided Layerwise Hybrid Attention），基于层间异质性洞察：早期层对softmax移除敏感，深层层存在冗余可被线性替代。方法为每层自适应平衡线性递归与可变大小滑动窗口softmax，实现非均匀压缩softmax覆盖面。

Result: 实验表明GLIDE在长上下文生成中实现了优越的性能-效率权衡，在不牺牲质量的前提下降低了端到端延迟。

Conclusion: 分层非均匀地压缩softmax注意力是兼顾KV缓存效率与模型表达能力的有效策略，验证了不同层对注意力机制敏感度存在差异这一假设。

Abstract: As Large Language Models scale to increasingly long contexts, the memory I/O and computational overhead of the Key-Value (KV) cache during decoding emerges as the primary throughput bottleneck. To address this, we propose GLIDE, a Guided Layerwise Hybrid Attention that strategically integrates sliding-window softmax attention with linear recurrent aggregation. GLIDE is motivated by layer-wise heterogeneity: early layers exhibit high sensitivity to softmax removal, while deeper layers demonstrate redundancy and tolerate aggressive replacement by linear alternatives. Leveraging this insight, GLIDE introduces a layer-wise adaptive mechanism wherein each layer balances an efficient linear recurrence with a variable-sized softmax window. Unlike uniform hybrid approaches, GLIDE non-uniformly compresses the softmax footprint across the model, reducing aggregate KV cache I/O while preserving expressive power where most vital. Empirical evaluations demonstrate the GLIDE achieves superior performance-efficiency tradeoffs, reducing end-to-end latency for long-context generation without compromising quality.

</details>


### [142] [A GAN-Based Framework for Robust Data Synthesis in Satellite Internet Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24790)
*Xiang Shi,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于生成式AI（GenAI）的框架，用于从不完整的低轨卫星互联网观测数据中合成高保真数据，实验证明GT-GAN模型在数据缺失（高达40%）场景下表现最优且鲁棒性最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前低轨卫星互联网观测数据常存在数据缺失问题，导致数据增强困难且代表性数据集受限，而生成式AI在该领域的应用尚缺乏研究。

Method: 提出基于GAN和VAE的GenAI框架，设计块缺失和点缺失两种模拟真实数据丢失场景，在WetLinks数据集上评估多种最新生成模型性能。

Result: GAN框架有效，GT-GAN在所有模型中表现最佳；即使在40%数据极端缺失条件下，GT-GAN仍具最高鲁棒性，能稳定捕获底层数据分布且泛化能力受影响最小。

Conclusion: 为基于GenAI的卫星网络数据增强和数据驱动研究指明了未来方向。

Abstract: Low-Earth orbit (LEO) satellite Internet has become an important infrastructure for enabling ubiquitous connectivity to align with the International Telecommunications Union vision for 6G telecommunications networks. However, current LEO satellite Internet observations often suffer from missing data, which complicates data augmentation task and limits the expansion of representative datasets. Given the complex characteristics of these datasets, generative AI (GenAI) presents a promising approach, yet its application in this domain has received little attention to date. In this paper, we propose a GenAI-based framework to synthesize high-fidelity data directly from incomplete LEO network observations. We propose the representative data missing scenarios, and evaluate the performance with the latest GAN- and VAE-based GenAI models on the recent WetLinks dataset. We design block-wise and point-wise missing scenarios to closely simulate the data loss that happens on real-world LEO satellite networks. Our results show the effectiveness of our proposed GAN-based framework and GT-GAN model exhibits the best performance among all models in both missing scenarios. Even under extreme conditions (e.g., 40% of the input data is missing), GT-GAN shows the highest robustness, consistently capturing the underlying input data distribution and being the least affected in terms of generalization. Our results shed light on future directions for GenAI-based data augmentation methods and data-driven research on satellite network measurement.

</details>


### [143] [Reasoning with Memory: A Temporal Granularity-Adaptive Framework for Training-Free Long Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24794)
*Linghao Meng,Qiankun Li,Junyuan Mao,Pujin Liao,Zhicheng He,Enbo Zhang,Kun Wang,Yang Liu,Huazhu Fu,Yueming Jin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ReMem，一种面向训练-free长视频问答的时域粒度自适应关键帧选择框架。通过双层记忆增强机制（查询层：利用LLM长时记忆解析问题时域粒度；视频层：利用帧结构记忆对齐语义并动态分配采样预算），在保持零样本能力的同时，显著提升MLLMs在长视频理解任务上的性能。在LVBench和LongVideoBench上分别取得+12.3%和+8.2%的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLMs受限于上下文窗口，无法直接处理长视频。主流的关键帧选择方法（如均匀采样或静态查询引导）未能适应查询的时域粒度变化，常忽略关键时序上下文，导致长视频问答性能受限。

Method: 提出双级记忆增强框架ReMem：1) 查询级：Memory-Driven Question Parsing利用LLM长时记忆解码问题时域粒度并提取语义实体；2) 视频级：Synergistic Dual-Semantic Frame Alignment利用帧内在结构记忆对齐语义，再通过Structure-Aware Dynamic Frame Routing聚类事件并动态分配采样预算。整个流程无需训练。

Result: 在4个长视频问答基准上使用3种MLLMs进行评估，达到零样本SOTA。其中LLaVA-Video+ReMem在LVBench达54.5%（+12.3%），在LongVideoBench达67.1%（+8.2%）。

Conclusion: 通过记忆机制显式保留时序信息，ReMem有效抑制冗余并赋予MLLMs多粒度视频推理能力，证明了训练-free的时域自适应关键帧选择在长视频理解中的可行性及优越性。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) demonstrate superior generalization in fundamental video tasks, restricted context windows limit their long video understanding. To accommodate this constraint, models typically resort to keyframe selection. However, uniform sampling or static query-guided selection often overlooks critical temporal context, failing to adapt to the varying query temporal granularities. In this paper, we propose ReMem, a temporal granularity-adaptive keyframe selection framework for training-free LongVideoQA. ReMem introduces a dual-level memory-augmented adaptation. At the query level, Memory-Driven Question Parsing leverages LLM long-term memory to decode question temporal granularity and extract semantic entities. At the video level, Synergistic Dual-Semantic Frame Alignment exploits intrinsic structural memory to align frames with query semantics, guiding Structure-Aware Dynamic Frame Routing to cluster events and optimally distribute sampling budgets. By explicitly preserving temporal information with memory mechanisms, ReMem suppresses redundancy and empowers MLLMs to perform robust multi-granular video reasoning. Evaluations across four popular LongVideoQA benchmarks using three MLLMs demonstrate highly efficient, state-of-the-art zero-shot performance; notably, LLaVA-Video with ReMem reaches 54.5% (+12.3%) on LVBench and 67.1% (+8.2%) on LongVideoBench.

</details>


### [144] [When Shortest Isn't Safest: A Design Science Approach to Senior-Friendly Pedestrian Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24795)
*Erdi Ünal,Daniel Eisenhardt,Christian Meske,Seyed Nima Afzali,Aysegül Dogangün*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过设计科学研究方法，基于老年人流动性约束，开发了面向老年人的友好型行人路径规划系统，并在实地行走研究中验证了其优于传统路径方案的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有行人导航系统主要优化距离或时间，忽视了影响老年人步行决策的障碍物、安全阈值和支持性基础设施，缺乏对老年人特殊需求的考量。

Method: 采用分层设计科学研究方法：通过11次半结构化访谈提取设计需求与设计原则，在OpenStreetMap上构建包含便利设施（长椅、厕所、遮蔽处）和高程数据的行人网络，实现基于A*算法的可配置成本路由引擎。通过14名老年人的实地行走研究进行验证评估。

Result: 老年人对友好型路径的总体偏好更高；主题分析表明基础设施维护、季节条件、交通暴露度和社会背景影响路径接受度；提炼出强调情境感知风险建模、多路线透明性、地标解释、社会情境敏感性和阶段适当信息等改进的设计需求与设计原则。

Conclusion: 本文为开发面向老年人的行人导航系统提供了可操作的设计指导，强调了将真实世界流动性约束转化为规范性设计知识的重要性。
